      277 +### 2026-09-30 — D16: Use whichever API key is entered (UI key
          + entry and `RAG_LLM_PROVIDER=auto`)                           
      278 +- **Context:** Requested: let the user enter an Anthropic or O
          +penAI API key, and have the                                   
      279 +  application use whichever one was entered.                  
      280 +- **Options:**                                                
      281 +  1. A "set key" endpoint that swaps the server's LLM globally
          +.                                                             
      282 +  2. **Request-scoped keys**: the UI sends the key with each r
          +equest, and the server                                        
      283 +     builds that provider for the request.                    
      284 +  3. Writing the key into `.env` from the UI.                 
      285 +- **Decision:** Option 2, plus auto-detection:                
      286 +  - `sk-ant-…` means Anthropic; any other `sk-…` means OpenAI.
          + A provider selector                                          
      287 +    overrides the guess.                                      
      288 +  - New headers `X-LLM-API-Key`, `X-LLM-Provider` and `X-LLM-M
          +odel` on `/query`,                                            
      289 +    `/evaluate` and `/llm/verify`.                            
      290 +  - The same rule applies to server configuration: the new def
          +ault                                                          
      291 +    `RAG_LLM_PROVIDER=auto` uses whichever of `ANTHROPIC_API_K
          +EY` / `OPENAI_API_KEY` is                                     
      292 +    set (Anthropic first), and falls back to retrieval-only if
          + neither is set.                                              
      293 +- **Reason:** Option 1 makes one user's key everyone's, and ad
          +ds shared mutable state and                                   
      294 +  races. Option 3 persists secrets from a web form. With optio
          +n 2, keys live only in the                                    
      295 +  user's session, and the server stays stateless about credent
          +ials.                                                         
      296 +- **Trade-offs:**                                             
      297 +  - The key crosses the UI→API hop on every request. That's pl
          +ain HTTP, acceptable on                                       
      298 +    localhost only; put TLS in front for remote use (09_SECURI
          +TY).                                                          
      299 +  - `auto` only sees keys in the environment or `.env`. Anthro
          +pic credentials from an                                       
      300 +    `ant auth login` profile need an explicit `RAG_LLM_PROVIDE
          +R=anthropic`.                                                 
      301 +  - With no key, the default moved from "anthropic, then an er
          +ror on first question"                                        
      302 +    to "retrieval-only", which is friendlier. The explicit err
          +or path (D13) still applies                                   
      303 +    when a provider is forced.                                
      304 +  - `RAG_ALLOW_CLIENT_LLM_KEYS=false` disables client keys for
          + shared deployments.                                          
      305 +- **Bugs caught while building it:**                          
      306 +  - My settings edit accidentally moved `collections_dir` out 
          +of the `Settings` class,                                      
      307 +    and every API test failed. It was fixed immediately.      
      308 +  - FastAPI couldn't resolve a dependency alias defined inside
          + `create_app` (because of                                     
      309 +    postponed annotations), so it silently treated the depende
          +ncy as a query parameter                                      
      310 +    and every query returned 422. The dependency now lives at 
          +module level.

● You can now enter either an Anthropic or an OpenAI API key, and the app
  answers with whichever one you entered.

  In the web UI (sidebar → "Answer model"):
  1. Choose My API key (it's pre-selected when the server has no key of its
     own).
  2. Paste your key. The provider is detected from its format: sk-ant-… is
     Anthropic, and any other sk-… is OpenAI. The sidebar shows e.g. "Using
     OpenAI · gpt-5.5 · key sk-proj…1234".
  3. If you want, override the provider or type a model name, then press Check
     key. It makes one tiny call and reports "Key works" or "OpenAI rejected
     this API key".
  4. Ask questions as before. Remove the key, or switch back to Server default,
     to go back to the server's configuration.

  Through server configuration (.env or environment): the new default,
  RAG_LLM_PROVIDER=auto, uses whichever key is set. Anthropic wins if both are
  set, and with neither the app answers in retrieval-only mode instead of
  erroring. You can still force anthropic, openai or none. If your Anthropic
  login comes from ant auth login rather than a key, set
  RAG_LLM_PROVIDER=anthropic explicitly.

  How the key is handled: it stays in your browser session and is sent to the
  API with each question. The server uses it only for that request and never
  saves or logs it, and a test checks it appears in no response or log. The UI
  and API talk plain HTTP, which is fine on your own machine. Put TLS in front
  if they run on different machines. RAG_ALLOW_CLIENT_LLM_KEYS=false switches
  key entry off on a shared server.

  Verified:
  - 261 offline tests and the 2 real-model tests pass, and lint is clean.
  - The UI tests type a key into the sidebar and get a cited answer back through
    the real API.
  - A real run used deliberately invalid keys against both vendors. Each was
    routed to the right vendor, rejected by that vendor's real API, and reported
    in the UI, and the key never appeared in the server log. I still haven't
    seen a real answer from either provider, since I have no valid key. Your
    first question with a real key will be the first true end-to-end test.

  Bug fixed that affects you: in .env.example, a line like ANTHROPIC_API_KEY=
  # comment was read as a key whose value is the comment text. That's been there
  since the OpenAI change. With auto it would have silently picked Anthropic
  with a bogus key for anyone copying the file. The comments are now on their
  own lines, and blank or #… values count as no key.

  To pick this up: restart both processes, rag serve and rag ui (or just rag ui
  --with-api).

  Still uncommitted. The UI and key work share pyproject.toml, uv.lock and
  several docs with the parallel CSV/XLSX/PPTX parser work. Tell me whether to
  commit everything together or only my changes.
